- **jira-ticket-planner**: Use this agent when you need to create a planning document from a Jira ticket. This agent retrieves ticket details, analyzes requirements, and produces comprehensive documentation without implementing anything. ([bildzeitung/job-harness](https://github.com/bildzeitung/job-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/job-preparer.md) · ★2)
- **job-profile**: Builds the candidate profile for a job search from the user's own CV: consolidated profile, one-page base CV and LinkedIn texts written for recruiters. It launches jobs, waits for them (pace-line resumes it with each alert for its jobs), chains the steps the brief lists, retries trivial failures, collects metrics and writes the factual report. Use it for a sweep, a chain of runs, or any launch-and-wait work. In the brief, state what to run, what it may change on its own (configs, seeds, a sweep rule), when to stop, and what report to write. It stops and reports on anything outside the brief. ([didac-alonso/didac-mods](https://github.com/didac-alonso/didac-mods/blob/HEAD/plugins/pace-line/agents/job-runner.md))

([CrocSwap/fadeno](https://github.com/CrocSwap/fadeno/blob/HEAD/plugin-omp/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Evaluate skill execution outputs using binary judgment and soft scoring. ([cxm95/security-agent-orchestrator](https://github.com/cxm95/security-agent-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/evo-skills/secskill-evo/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Evaluate skill execution outputs to diagnose failures and guide conservative evolution. ([cxm95/security-agent-orchestrator](https://github.com/cxm95/security-agent-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/evo-skills/secskill-evo-neo/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Rules on the operator's behalf on a product question (a `[proposed]` "What the student will see" line, a decisions card, a round's found-case question) ONLY when a dated precedent of his covers it, quoting that precedent verbatim; otherwise escalates it to him. Give it the question and its options, never the session's recommendation or the fix. Returns one JSON verdict per question. It never edits files, labels or issues — the calling session records the verdict with `node scripts/judgments.mjs record`. Built for ([dcodish/geo_builder](https://github.com/dcodish/geo_builder/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Weighs the advocates' arguments against the code itself, picks a design, writes an ADR and updates PLAN.md. Use after the debate stage. Also rules on test disputes between the implementer and a test in unattended runs. ([doug-patterson/agent-pipeline](https://github.com/doug-patterson/agent-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Juzga con una rúbrica fijada de antemano, o intenta refutar un hallazgo, en la validación de un caso de adw. Solo lo lanza el coordinador de adw. ([eLafo/desarrollo-agentico-todo-07](https://github.com/eLafo/desarrollo-agentico-todo-07/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Judgment tier (Opus, high effort, no file edits). Use for code review, adversarial critique, security or design calls, verifying another agent's work, and any task where a wrong answer is costly and hard to detect. Judgment work always comes here; it is never sent to a cheaper tier. ([EONRaider/agent-router](https://github.com/EONRaider/agent-router/blob/HEAD/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Weighs a review round's Blocking and Non-blocking findings against what the project is for. Give it the findings (file:line, category), the plan path, loc/PRD.md and CLAUDE.md. Per finding it says "fix", "enough" or "challenges D-NN" with a reason. It never edits files or adds findings. ([etobod/masterskills](https://github.com/etobod/masterskills/blob/HEAD/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Combines Enigma oracle verdicts into one evidence-gated assessment. Dispatched by Enigma commands with oracle verdicts and an evidence bundle; not for direct user invocation. ([glapsfun/enigma](https://github.com/glapsfun/enigma/blob/HEAD/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Reward-hack judge. Reads .harness/spec.md and git diff, issues AGREE / DISAGREE verdict on whether the diff faithfully implements the spec without gaming the tests. Invoked from Stop hook (Claude fallback path only). ([good-night-oppie/agentdex](https://github.com/good-night-oppie/agentdex/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Checks every board entry of a round against the code with a fixed rubric; marks false, unsure and duplicate entries. Run after the reviewers, before the index. ([guilherme-luiz-cella/lean-agent-revie](https://github.com/guilherme-luiz-cella/lean-agent-revie/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Pixel Perfect's packaged judge. Compares one batch of rendered components to their design frames and returns a JSON verdict for each. Started only by Pixel Perfect workflows, with a brief from scripts/brief.mjs judge. ([hackerpug-ai/pixel-perfect](https://github.com/hackerpug-ai/pixel-perfect/blob/HEAD/plugins/pixel-perfect/agents/judge.md))
- **Judge**: コードレビュー・バグ検出・品質評価。コード修正はしない。 ([hinominant/cc-orchestration-workshop](https://github.com/hinominant/cc-orchestration-workshop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Adversarial reviewer locked to one lens. Tries to falsify a spec, plan or diff against live code and returns a graded verdict. Use for the pre-build panel, per-batch review and the integrated review. ([Insynq/agent-blueprint](https://github.com/Insynq/agent-blueprint/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Read-only subagent for subjective judgment against a rubric. Returns structured scores + justifications. ([jaredwins99/llm-reproducibility-hooks](https://github.com/jaredwins99/llm-reproducibility-hooks/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Objectively evaluate and compare two competing plugin implementations using a weighted rubric ([JayKim88/claude-ai-engineering](https://github.com/JayKim88/claude-ai-engineering/blob/HEAD/plugins/competitive-agents/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Adversarial verifier for correctness-critical claims — "is this bug real?", "does this fix actually hold?", "is this finding refutable?". Read-only; verdicts with evidence. Use AFTER scouts/workers/builders produce findings or fixes, before anything is treated as true. Expensive — send it claims worth judging, not bulk work. ([jeewandaniel/claude-delegation-framework](https://github.com/jeewandaniel/claude-delegation-framework/blob/HEAD/agents/judge.md) · 2 copies)
- **judge**: Final adjudicator for /debate. Reads the converged debate state — every finding, its status, and the full attack/defense history — and renders a verdict: the stronger side with grounded reasoning, or a justified crux when the disagreement is genuinely irreducible. Writes JSON to a temp file. ([jjcfatras/Claude](https://github.com/jjcfatras/Claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/debate/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Judgment work a jstack skill hands off - a design call, a review, an explanation, a synthesis, or the hardest code. Its model is set in this file, so don't pass one. ([joaocarloscruz/jstack](https://github.com/joaocarloscruz/jstack/blob/HEAD/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Validates findings from two independent code reviewers against the actual code, deduplicates, and returns structured results. Dispatched by the review orchestrator, not invoked directly. ([joemiller/claude-plugins](https://github.com/joemiller/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/review/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Independent, read-only evidence judge for a bountyproof engagement. Decides whether a validated finding has enough proof to become a report (PASS / NEEDS_VALIDATION / DISCARD). Use in Phase 3b and as the second opinion in Phase 4c. ([jorge-asenjo/bountyproof](https://github.com/jorge-asenjo/bountyproof/blob/HEAD/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Adjudication tier (Opus, xhigh effort). Use only for contested calls where being wrong is expensive and cheaper tiers have already disagreed or already failed — refuting a claimed finding, choosing between competing designs, deciding whether a risky change is safe, settling a split verdict from a verifier panel. Has no Edit or Write tools. Do NOT use as a general-purpose worker: Opus at xhigh was the slowest and most expensive cell measured, about six times the time and cost of `worker` on bounded work for the same score, so routine work here is the commonest way to waste both. ([joshuawowk/intelligent_delegate](https://github.com/joshuawowk/intelligent_delegate/blob/HEAD/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: 결재 담당(Opus). 중요한 일의 계획 승인, 큰 결정, 최종 결과 판정만 맡는다. 되돌리기 어렵거나, 바깥으로 나가거나, 돈이 들거나, 큰 작업일 때만 부른다. 일상 작업에는 부르지 않는다(사용량을 많이 쓴다). Use only for high-stakes plan approval, decisions and final sign-off. ([joymin5655/pro-agent](https://github.com/joymin5655/pro-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: The fresh-context stop-condition evaluator. Use once per card, after the Skeptic has verified, to decide PASS or REJECT against the card's stated goal. Deliberately not a Council seat; shares no context with the generators or the owner. ([jumpseat-inc/pi-council](https://github.com/jumpseat-inc/pi-council/blob/HEAD/council/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Read-only review of the diff against the spec - scope creep, over-engineering, test tampering, visual drift. Posts one PR review comment. ([jwheatp/tofu](https://github.com/jwheatp/tofu/blob/HEAD/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: 工业诊断流程Step 5 — 质量门审查。评分10项标准，验证诊断推理与统计基础的完整性，输出pass/needs_repair/fail。 ([kingdol666/online-film-optimizer](https://github.com/kingdol666/online-film-optimizer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: 第二意見與仲裁者。三種時機用它：(1) 高風險判斷（金流、庫存、auth、RLS、架構取捨）要第二意見；(2) 多個候選方案要評審選優；(3) 升級路徑到頂（opus 實作也失敗）要診斷卡點。給它問題全文＋候選方案或失敗軌跡，它獨立分析後給裁決與理由。它不負責實作。 ([KuanYuJiangTW/my-tea-shop](https://github.com/KuanYuJiangTW/my-tea-shop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: 行政(実行)から分離された独立検証エージェント(司法)。実装の成果物を目標契約に照らし、 決定性チェック(テスト/CI/型/lint の機械判定)とセマンティックチェック(LLM)の二系統で 独立採点し、PASS / REJECT / RETRY / ESCALATE の判定を返す。次のとき招集すること: 実装完了後の最終判定、フル自律ループの停止条件判定、"done" の真偽確認、PR 作成可否の ゲート。コードは書かない・直さない(行政との分離の担保)。決定性グリーン無しに PASS しない。 証拠が無ければ PASS せず RETRY。コード品質の各観点別レビューは対象外(それは行政側の pr-review-toolkit が「証拠提供=書記」として担い、本エージェントはその所見を束ねて判定する判事)。 ([MacoTasu/agent-skills](https://github.com/MacoTasu/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/review-judge/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Final filter for all PR review comments. Applies 4-gate filter: Accuracy + Actionability + Novelty + Specificity. Only agent that posts to GitHub. ([Manolii-org/ai-starter-pack](https://github.com/Manolii-org/ai-starter-pack/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge.md) · 3 copies)
- **judge**: Scores competing candidate solutions and picks or synthesizes the best. Use in best-of-N / debate patterns to arbitrate against an objective rubric. ([mdrewt/monster-realm](https://github.com/mdrewt/monster-realm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Use to grade neutral-labelled outputs against a rubric, blind, with a yes or no and reasoning for each claim. ([melandres8/hidkit](https://github.com/melandres8/hidkit/blob/HEAD/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Impartial arbiter who assesses queries, evaluates arguments, verifies claims, and controls debate termination ([miiraheart/claude-debate](https://github.com/miiraheart/claude-debate/blob/HEAD/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Author-blind second opinion on an idea evaluation. Reads only the card, answer cards, dossier, law check, kill check, reconcile and audit files plus the kill-pattern reference; forms its own pattern opinion and verdict (DEAD, ALIVE or INSUFFICIENT_DATA) before seeing the conductor's; compares branch variants pairwise with the order swapped. Writes judge.json. No web access. ([mikamika06/idea-evaluator](https://github.com/mikamika06/idea-evaluator/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: 難易度「最高」の判断 — 判断が割れる設計の比較、根拠の弱い主張の検出、決定ログや報告の監査、spec の代替案出し。調査そのものには investigate-deep、手順どおりの検証には verify。 ([mizoo-snow21/dotfiles](https://github.com/mizoo-snow21/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Judges one question for an swd principal that is not conducting on Fable 5.2, such as the plan, a ruling at a trust boundary or a contested ruling. Reads the run folder and the project, runs nothing, and writes one report whose first line is its verdict. The principal dispatches it with the model set to fable. ([parthadcdev/swd-agents](https://github.com/parthadcdev/swd-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Separate-context mutation-judge. Classifies surviving mutants from the C.1 mutation harness as `equivalent` (no test could ever distinguish the mutant from the original) or `genuine` (a future test could kill it). Spawned by the root orchestrator from the C.1 packet — Claude Code subagents cannot spawn other subagents, so the harness writes a judge-packet to disk and the orchestrator relays it. Never auto-routed. ([preql-data/claude-workflow-plugin](https://github.com/preql-data/claude-workflow-plugin/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Executes one judgment ticket of musthold-dp as a blind executor. Launch it only through the musthold-dp-fulfil skill, with a prompt that is the ticket. ([RachidChabane/musthold](https://github.com/RachidChabane/musthold/blob/HEAD/discpower/plugin/claude/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Executes one judgment ticket of musthold-alignment as a blind executor. Launch it only through the musthold-alignment-fulfil skill, with a prompt that is the ticket. ([RachidChabane/musthold](https://github.com/RachidChabane/musthold/blob/HEAD/alignment/plugin/claude/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Runs one match on the held-out evaluation set with one blue policy and one red policy, verifies it against the four validity checks, and reports what happened including failure. Not rated, produces no research, changes nothing. ([rapturt9/aar-forum-elo](https://github.com/rapturt9/aar-forum-elo/blob/HEAD/scaffolds/judge-base/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Адверсариальный судья. Выносит вердикт PASS/FAIL по задаче на этапе verify. Вердикт ДЕТЕРМИНИРОВАН — опирается на объективные гейты и находки тестеров, а не на «мнение модели». Использовать в конце итерации, когда реализация заявлена готовой. ([rayne-ai/demo-factory](https://github.com/rayne-ai/demo-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Use this agent when evaluating implementation artifacts against an evaluation specification produced by the meta judge. Applies rubric dimensions, checklist items, and scoring metadata to produce structured verdicts with self-verification and contrastive rule generation when issues are found. ([realgammer10-alt/Context-Engineering-Kit-Master](https://github.com/realgammer10-alt/Context-Engineering-Kit-Master/blob/HEAD/context-engineering-kit-master/plugins/sadd/agents/judge.md) · 4 copies)
- **judge**: Labels one batch of prompts for the is-it-nerfed skill. Use it only when the is-it-nerfed skill starts it. ([resitaydin/is-it-nerfed](https://github.com/resitaydin/is-it-nerfed/blob/HEAD/plugins/is-it-nerfed/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Judge Agent — 심사위원 시뮬레이션, 치명적 약점 지적, 점수 예측 ([River-181/KEG-Mangsanggwedo](https://github.com/River-181/KEG-Mangsanggwedo/blob/HEAD/.agent/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Adjudicates dispositions vs findings (default), or TODO acceptability for burndown (todo-burndown mode). Fresh per phase. ([rnortman/claude-plugins](https://github.com/rnortman/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/review-chain/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Fresh-context judge for one factory ticket: scores the rubric rows over the frozen evidence and returns pass or fail with fixes. ([SamyakJhaveri/loam](https://github.com/SamyakJhaveri/loam/blob/HEAD/cultivation/marketplace/sam-cc-setup/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: One judge on a Bishop & Barrister challenge panel. Launched only by the bishop-and-barrister skill's challenge procedure (CHALLENGE.md), one agent per judge; never for ordinary rules questions. ([smithy-org/bishop-and-barrister](https://github.com/smithy-org/bishop-and-barrister/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Scores arena bout candidates against a rubric they never saw and recommends a base. Read-only. Use from /arena:bout once every candidate has finished. ([spartDev/beads-arena](https://github.com/spartDev/beads-arena/blob/HEAD/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Reflection judge for email-summarize run transcripts. Use when reviewing a run transcript against docs/rubric-email-summarize.md. ([swarnaburra/Unit-2-Project-Fashion-Mate](https://github.com/swarnaburra/Unit-2-Project-Fashion-Mate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Judges one of a run's proposals against the corpus, as a judge, a group judge or a final judge. Spawned only by the elucidate skill's step 2; not for general work. ([threldhq/reqctl](https://github.com/threldhq/reqctl/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Rules on contested verdicts and synthesises the playbook — decides whether an accumulated set of experiments justifies promoting a rule, and whether existing rules still hold. Invoke rarely, for decisions whose cost of error is measured in months. ([tka4enko/ads-discipline](https://github.com/tka4enko/ads-discipline/blob/HEAD/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Judges the shots one proof round marks judged against the approved boards, the design docs, both rulings lists and honesty on screen; writes a numbered verdict on the PR and sends VERDICT to the head. Fresh per round, none on a round that only repairs what CI lists as broken and changes no behaviour; any surface; never reads code. ([tsomaia-me/metaharness](https://github.com/tsomaia-me/metaharness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Assess implement-review loop meta-health and return continue, rotate-implementer, or escalate with a written rationale ([weftwiseink/clauthier](https://github.com/weftwiseink/clauthier/blob/HEAD/plugins/cdocs/agents/judge.md))
- **judge**: Scores one ticket's condensed transcripts and ticket files against the doctor rubrics (efficiency, code quality, workflow compliance, communication, tix checks K1-K9) and lists the failures behind each lowered score, with evidence and the tix skill, repo or prompting habit that owns each. Writes only its score.md. Spawned by /tix:doctor. ([zero4573/tix](https://github.com/zero4573/tix/blob/HEAD/assets/plugin/agents/judge.md))
- **Judge Haiku**: Outcome evaluator (Haiku tier). Fast, differently-calibrated judge providing a third independent vote. Champion is the score-5 reference. Samples 3 times, reports median. ([lenulus/deep_research](https://github.com/lenulus/deep_research/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge_haiku.md))
- **Judge Opus**: Outcome evaluator (Opus tier). Deep-reasoning judge for complex analytical quality signals. Champion is the score-5 reference. Samples 3 times, reports median. ([lenulus/deep_research](https://github.com/lenulus/deep_research/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge_opus.md))
- **Judge Sonnet**: Outcome evaluator (Sonnet tier). Scores a candidate against the rubric using Prometheus-style anchored scoring. Champion is the score-5 reference. Samples 3 times, reports median. ([lenulus/deep_research](https://github.com/lenulus/deep_research/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge_sonnet.md))
- **judge-a**: Adversarial code reviewer — blind judge A for the dual review mode of construct:review. Launched by the orchestrator when the user asks for a dual or adversarial review. ([carsimax/construct](https://github.com/carsimax/construct/blob/HEAD/plugins/construct/agents/judge-a.md) · ★1)
- **judge-agent**: Use PROACTIVELY when gaslighter:judge delegates workspace scoring. Judges eval workspaces for completeness and overcorrection against rubrics. ([LarryGF/gaslighter](https://github.com/LarryGF/gaslighter/blob/HEAD/agents/judge-agent.md) · ★5)
- **judge-agent**: Use this agent to review borderline attack results where pattern matching and LLM judge disagree or confidence is low. Also triggered when user questions a specific verdict or asks to re-evaluate findings. ([allsmog/promptarmor-plugin](https://github.com/allsmog/promptarmor-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/judge-agent.md) · ★1)
- **judge-agent**: Read-only quality gate — workflow, security, tests, domain, Design Contract, and UI DESIGN-GATE / anti-slop rubric. ([phuoctrung-ppt/ai-sdlc-workflow](https://github.com/phuoctrung-ppt/ai-sdlc-workflow/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/judge-agent.md) · ★1)
- **judge-agent** ([hts2008/edu_manager_v2](https://github.com/hts2008/edu_manager_v2/blob/HEAD/.agent/agents/judge-agent.md))
- **judge-agent**: Provide independent, adversarial review of code and decisions produced by other agents. You are the impartial third party — you did not write the code, you have no stake in the approach, and you evaluate purely on correctness, maintainability, security, and adherence to project standards. When you disagree with an approach, you provide evidence and alternatives. ([hts2008/edu_manager_v2](https://github.com/hts2008/edu_manager_v2/blob/HEAD/agents/judge-agent.md))
- **judge-agent**: Use this agent when an AI agent output needs to be evaluated for quality. This agent acts as an independent LLM-as-Judge, scoring outputs on a G-Eval-inspired rubric and returning structured critique for retry injection. Examples: ([jamjahal/verdict](https://github.com/jamjahal/verdict/blob/HEAD/agents/judge-agent.md))
- **judge-agent**: Quality gate for all problem outputs. Runs linting (ruff), type checking (mypy), schema validation, and smoke tests against the generated application. Posts a PASS or FAIL verdict to judge_verdicts. A task CANNOT be marked completed without a PASS verdict. Invoke after software-architect has completed the synthesise task. ([SharathSPhD/oak](https://github.com/SharathSPhD/oak/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge-agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **judge-anthropic**: Phase 2 of the LLM benchmark split pipeline — evaluate model responses using Claude Sonnet 5 (Anthropic) as LLM-as-a-Judge. Run AFTER `make collect` and BEFORE `make merge`. ([Palo-IT-GitHub-Demos/model-compass](https://github.com/Palo-IT-GitHub-Demos/model-compass/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/judge-anthropic.agent.md))
- **judge-astro**: 独立检查本案占星原始 JSON 的计算问题，并依据公开方法合同独立把这张盘读一遍，给出自己的结论。只处理 astrology 维度，不评价 analyst、不比较成稿、不裁定一致性。你的独立读法是 chief 用来对照 analyst 的另一份专业意见，所以要像一位占星师那样下判断，而不是只复述位置。 ([Loveacup/jz-skills](https://github.com/Loveacup/jz-skills/blob/HEAD/shared/destiny-matrix/agents/judge-astro.md) · ★1)
- **judge-b**: Adversarial code reviewer — blind judge B for the dual review mode of construct:review. Launched by the orchestrator when the user asks for a dual or adversarial review. ([carsimax/construct](https://github.com/carsimax/construct/blob/HEAD/plugins/construct/agents/judge-b.md) · ★1)
- **judge-bazi**: 只凭本案八字原始 JSON 与公开方法合同，独立完成两件事：检查计算问题；按子平格局法独立断出这张盘的旺衰、格局、用神与喜忌等结论。你看不到 analyst 的结论，你的读法是 chief 用来对照的第二份独立判断，所以每一项都要给出明确结论，chief 才有东西可比。只处理 bazi 维度，不评价 analyst、不比较成稿。 ([Loveacup/jz-skills](https://github.com/Loveacup/jz-skills/blob/HEAD/shared/destiny-matrix/agents/judge-bazi.md) · ★1)
- **judge-calibration**: Use this agent when a model-based judge's scores need to be validated against real human judgment before being trusted for gating decisions (CI gates, cost-optimization experiments). It runs the full human-vs-judge kappa loop — sampling, generating a human scoring sheet, pausing for real human input, computing Cohen's kappa, diagnosing disagreements, editing the judge prompt, and re-running — up to 3 iterations before stopping and reporting a persistent pattern instead of looping forever. ([agenticgogol/Edureka_Coding_Agent_Enabled_Demo_11Jul](https://github.com/agenticgogol/Edureka_Coding_Agent_Enabled_Demo_11Jul/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge-calibration.md) · ★1)
- **judge-codex-jury**: Proactively use when the user runs any `/judge-codex:*` command. Thin forwarding wrapper that hands a `plan` cycle artifact (blueprint / plan / implementation log / review report) plus its golden-rule contract to Codex via the companion script. Returns Codex stdout verbatim. ([usetheodev/judge-codex](https://github.com/usetheodev/judge-codex/blob/HEAD/agents/judge-codex-jury.md))
- **judge-coordinator**: Phase 2 coordinator — dispatches the 3 blind judge agents (Anthropic, OpenAI, Google) on every part of the latest judging batch, in parallel, and loops on `make check-judging` until every score file is valid and its model verified. Run AFTER `make collect` and BEFORE `make merge`. ([Palo-IT-GitHub-Demos/model-compass](https://github.com/Palo-IT-GitHub-Demos/model-compass/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/judge-coordinator.agent.md))
- **judge-correctness**: Independently judge whether the result satisfies the task brief. ([FledsonChagas/AutoCast](https://github.com/FledsonChagas/AutoCast/blob/HEAD/agents/judge-correctness.md) · ★2)
- **judge-council**: Reviews proposed changes to the tvl-tech-bias-validator's rubric or calibration. Designed to be spawned three times in parallel with different model tiers (opus, sonnet, haiku) so model diversity — not just role diversity — provides the independence. Each invocation produces one tier-verdict; the main session aggregates across tiers for the final decision. ([danlex/ethicalhive](https://github.com/danlex/ethicalhive/blob/HEAD/agents/judge-council.md) · ★1)
- **judge-critic**: Use when the team wants a skeptical HackUTD 2026 judge to tear into an idea, a scope plan, a demo script, a table pitch, or a Devpost write-up before real judges see it. Returns 1-5 scores per criterion, a plain top-3 verdict, the 3 biggest gaps with cited fixes, the 5 hardest judge questions, 4-minute table failure risks, and the one highest-leverage change. ([Proton-Designer/HackUTD26](https://github.com/Proton-Designer/HackUTD26/blob/HEAD/HackUTD-2026-Kit/.claude/agents/judge-critic.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **judge-cross-vendor**: Cross-vendor judge for Hydra attended engineering. Spawned by hydra.workflow.submit_host_result when gate_eligible_judges returns required_cross_vendor=true. ([lebobo88/Hydra](https://github.com/lebobo88/Hydra/blob/HEAD/plugins/hydra/agents/judge-cross-vendor.md) · ★4)

Must always be a different vendor from the stage's generator. Never dispatch this agent to judge its own prior output. ([lebobo88/fable-harness](https://github.com/lebobo88/fable-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge-cross-vendor.md))
- **judge-decision-maker**: Produces final decision from verified evidence, including uncertainties. ([gqy20/IssueLab](https://github.com/gqy20/IssueLab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/judge-decision-maker.md) · ★12)

- **judge-dredd**: Impartial read-only reviewer of the lead's work at the task checkpoints (plan, delivery, replies to a pull request review). - **judge-dredd**: Read-only reviewer subagent that judges the lead's plan (when the task changes a contract, the audio path, the guardrails or many files) and, once per task, its delivery before the pull request, from a brief built by a script and received as a path only; the guard and the Stop hook that enforce it, and their limits. - **judge-editor**: Stage 4 of the judge iteration loop. Editorial gate. Dispatched (often in parallel) by the Opus senior session. ([DevNullLtd/Yuzu](https://github.com/DevNullLtd/Yuzu/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/junior-developer.md) · ★19)
- **junior-developer**: PHẢI dùng agent này khi: task là CRUD đơn giản (create/read/update/delete với logic thẳng), UI cơ bản (form, list, dropdown) có spec rõ ràng không cần quyết định kiến trúc, bug fix P3 ≤5 dòng. KHÔNG dùng khi: task đụng auth/payment/real-time/core business logic (→ senior-developer), task cần quyết định về pattern hoặc architecture (→ tech-lead), cần review code người khác (→ senior-developer). - **junior-engineer**: A focused implementer for SMALL, well-scoped changes — the long tail of S-sized tasks: a copy/CSS tweak, adding one field to a model + its read/write, a one-line bug fix + its regression test, a single review-finding fix. - **junior-engineer**: Reads a plan, ticket, or task before work starts and reports every place an implementer would have to guess.
